- **coreex-adapter**: Create or modify a CoreEx Infrastructure-layer adapter (anti-corruption layer). USE FOR: new adapter interface in Application/Adapters/{ExternalDomain}/, new adapter implementation in Infrastructure/Adapters/{ExternalDomain}/, new typed HTTP client in Infrastructure/Clients/{ExternalDomain}/, event-driven sync/replication adapter (IXxxSyncAdapter), unit tests for HTTP clients with MockHttpClientFactory. DO NOT USE FOR: repositories within the same domain (use coreex-repository), application services that call adapters (use coreex-app-service), event subscriber hosts that drive sync adapters (… ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-adapter.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-aggregate**: Add or modify a DDD domain object in a CoreEx Domain layer. USE FOR: new aggregate root (Aggregate&lt;TId,TSelf&gt;), new child entity (Entity&lt;TId,TSelf&gt;) owned by an aggregate, new value object (sealed record), adding mutation methods, PersistenceState-aware factory methods, mutation guards (OnCheckCanMutate/OnMutate). Interviews the developer to determine which of the three (aggregate root / entity / value object) applies, then follows the appropriate pattern. DO NOT USE FOR: CRUD-oriented domains with no Domain layer (use coreex-app-service directly against repositories), Application-layer mappe… ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-aggregate.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-api**: Add or modify a CoreEx API controller (or Minimal API endpoint) in an *.Api host. USE FOR: scaffolding the MVC controller pair (XxxController + XxxReadController), GET/query/schema endpoints, POST create, PUT + PATCH full-entity update, DELETE, and custom business-action endpoints. Covers both exception-based and Result&lt;T&gt; service styles, and Minimal API as an alternative to MVC. DO NOT USE FOR: Api host setup / Program.cs (use coreex-scaffold), application services (use coreex-app-service), API integration tests (use coreex-test-api). ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-api.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-api-e2e**: Create a complete new entity with CRUD API endpoints end-to-end in a single guided workflow: DTO contract, database migration, EF Core repository, validator (+ unit tests), optional policy guard (+ unit tests), application service, API endpoint, and integration tests. USE FOR: adding a brand-new entity to an existing CoreEx solution where the full stack — from database table to HTTP endpoint — is needed. DO NOT USE FOR: modifying an existing entity or endpoint (use the targeted L1 skill directly), read-only façade entities backed by an external adapter with no local table (use individual L1 s… ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-api-e2e.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-app-service**: Create or modify a CoreEx Application-layer service. USE FOR: new service class (exception-based or Result&lt;T&gt;), adding CRUD/business operations, CQRS read service (XxxReadService), adapter interface in Application/Adapters/, policy class in Application/Policies/, application-level mapper (Domain → Contract). DO NOT USE FOR: Infrastructure repositories (use coreex-repository), validators (use coreex-validator), controller endpoints (use coreex-api). ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-app-service.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-aspire**: Add or extend .NET Aspire orchestration and end-to-end tests in a CoreEx solution. USE FOR: AppHost wiring for new hosts, service-bus queue/topic topology in Test.Aspire, MockHost stubs for third-party HTTP, OnBeforeStartAsync/OnAfterStartAsync setup, E2E tests across domains. DO NOT USE FOR: non-Aspire hosts (use coreex-scaffold) or per-host integration tests (use coreex-test-api / coreex-test-subscribe / coreex-test-relay). ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-aspire.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-bootstrap**: CoreEx Bootstrap — generates root guidance files for a blank CoreEx repo, then chains to /coreex-scaffold. ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-bootstrap.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-command-publish-e2e**: Add the sending/publishing of a command (not an event) to another domain end-to-end: adapter, transactional call site (outbox vs direct), NamedDestinationProvider queue routing, Service Bus topology, API/Relay tests and Aspire E2E. USE FOR: a domain must instruct another domain to do something. DO NOT USE FOR: publishing an event (use coreex-app-service), receiving a command (use coreex-command-subscribe-e2e), or synchronous request/response calls (use coreex-adapter). ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-command-publish-e2e.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-command-subscribe-e2e**: Add the receiving/handling of a command (not an event) end-to-end: contract, app service, subscriber, dedicated keyed command-queue receiver in the Subscribe host, Service Bus topology, Subscribe tests and Aspire E2E. USE FOR: this domain must act on a command addressed to it. DO NOT USE FOR: subscribing to an event (use coreex-subscriber-e2e), sending a command (use coreex-command-publish-e2e), or subscribing to another domain's command (never valid). ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-command-subscribe-e2e.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-contract**: Create or modify a hand-authored contract (DTO/entity) in a CoreEx domain. USE FOR: new root entity contract, new subordinate/request contract, modifying an existing contract (add property, add interface, wire ref-data), extracting a shared base class. DO NOT USE FOR: reference-data contracts (generated via coreex-refdata / *.CodeGen), Infrastructure persistence models (generated by *.Database CodeGen). ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-contract.md) · ★29)
- **coreex-db-migration**: Add or change a database table for a CoreEx domain. USE FOR: new transactional table, new reference-data table, altering an existing table (columns, indexes, constraints), or any other schema change (indexes, functions, stored procs). Scaffolds the correct migration script, updates dbex.yaml, applies the migration, and regenerates Infrastructure persistence models. DO NOT USE FOR: outbox provisioning (use dotnet run -- script outbox directly), seed data only changes (dotnet run -- Data), or CoreEx contract/service generation (that is *.CodeGen, not *.Database). ([Avanade/CoreEx](https://github.com/Avanade/CoreEx/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/coreex-db-migration.md) · ★29)
